# Medžuslovjansky matematičny kanon · OLP-0183

To jest polny aktualny kanon terminov, ktory jest použit v medžuslovjanskom izdanje Open Logic. Latinična forma jest redakcijno kanonična; kirilične formy sųt deterministično izvedene. Každy zapis pokazuje smysl, gramatiku, sravnitělne formy, dokazy, alternativy, uvěrjenost i revizijnu liniju.

English: This is the complete current terminology canon used by the Open Logic Interslavic edition. Latin is the editable canonical witness; Cyrillic is deterministic. Every entry exposes its sense, grammar, comparanda, evidence, alternatives, confidence and revision lineage.

**Kontrolny punkt / checkpoint:** OLP-0183 · 345 aktivnyh zapisov · 396 fizičnyh revizij · novy zapis OLISV-T0345.

[Ekspertna knjiga odluka / expert decision log](EXPERT_REVIEW_LOG_OLP0183.md) · [machine JSONL](EXPERT_REVIEW_LOG_OLP0183.jsonl)

## OLISV-T0237 · algebra.abelian-group

- **English lemma / sense:** abelian group — A group whose binary operation is commutative, named here as an example of a many-sorted object domain.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `Abelova grupa`; lemma `Abelova grupa`; feminine proper-name possessive phrase.
- **Use/inflection:** Genitive Abelovoj grupy; plural Abelove grupy. Preserve Abel as the eponym in citations.
- **Kirilična forma:** `Абелова група`
- **Rationale:** The direct mathematical group head combines with the officially described -ov possessive pattern for a one-word masculine eponym.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.86 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0237-r0001`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: абелева группа; uk: абелева група; pl: grupa abelowa; cs: Abelova grupa; bcs: Abelova grupa; Абелова група; bg: Абелева група.
- **Evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[8040]; dictionary_id=1787; lemma=grupa; gloss=group); ISV-GRAMMAR-ADJ (lines 43-45; SHA-256 184348005ACB532868DAEB35DBFB7A8E05A493D550B4D6FA2E1A3BCFC906D84F); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternatives:** komutativna grupa: A valid semantic synonym, but it replaces the source's eponym rather than translating it.; abeleva grupa: Less consistent with the established project and official -ov possessive pattern.
- **Uncertainty:** The complete eponymic phrase is editorially composed; capitalization and national adjective vowels vary.

## OLISV-T0186 · algebra.group

- **English lemma / sense:** group — A structure with an associative binary operation, an identity element, and inverses, here presented through a first-order axiom system.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `grupa`; lemma `grupa`; feminine noun.
- **Use/inflection:** Feminine noun; genitive grupy, plural grupy. In this formal context it names the algebraic structure, not an informal collection.
- **Kirilična forma:** `група`
- **Rationale:** Grupa is the exact frozen lemma and is transparent across all six comparison traditions; the current source fixes its algebraic rather than social sense.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.98 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0186-r0001`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: группа; uk: група; pl: grupa; cs: grupa (mat.); bcs: grupa; група; bg: група.
- **Evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[8040]; dictionary_id=1787; lemma=grupa; gloss=group); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternatives:** skupina: The native alternative is common in Czech and some South-Slavic contexts, but it is less uniform across the six traditions and the dictionary explicitly supplies grupa for mathematics.
- **Uncertainty:** The source gives a right-sided presentation of identity and inverse; the term remains the ordinary group concept under the displayed associativity axiom.

## OLISV-T0246 · algebra.homomorphism

- **English lemma / sense:** homomorphism — A structure-preserving map between algebraic or relational structures.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `homomorfizm`; lemma `homomorfizm`; masculine noun.
- **Use/inflection:** Genitive homomorfizma; use homomorfizmom after jest and homomorfizmy in the plural.
- **Kirilična forma:** `хомоморфизм`
- **Rationale:** The international Greek-derived form is unambiguous in algebra and is the natural name for the source's structure-preserving map. The record does not infer additional algebraic operations beyond the source's statement.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.82 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0246-r0002`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: гомоморфизм; uk: гомоморфізм; pl: homomorfizm; cs: homomorfismus; bcs: homomorfizam; хомоморфизам; bg: хомоморфизъм.

- **Alternatives:** sȯhranitelj struktury: Descriptive but not the established mathematical noun.; izomorfizm: Too strong: the source has not yet established bijectivity plus inverse preservation.
- **Uncertainty:** No direct frozen homomorphism row was found in the bounded dictionary; national inflection varies.; The r0001 record remains preserved as adverse provenance for the target/source locator mismatch.

## OLISV-T0252 · algebra.ordered-field

- **English lemma / sense:** ordered field — A field equipped with an order compatible with its addition and multiplication.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `uporędženo tělo`; lemma `uporędženo tělo`; neuter adjective-noun phrase.
- **Use/inflection:** Genitive uporędženogo těla; use polno uporędženo tělo for the complete ordered-field phrase.
- **Kirilična forma:** `упореджено тєло`
- **Rationale:** Tělo is the established Interslavic algebraic carrier for field, while the ordering component follows the pinned oporędčeny family. The source's phrase is reproduced as a descriptive example, not a full axiomatization.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.7 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0252-r0002`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: упорядоченное поле; uk: впорядковане поле; pl: ciało uporządkowane; cs: uspořádané těleso; bcs: uređeno polje; уређено поље; bg: наредено поле.

- **Alternatives:** uporędženo polje: Polje is a field component in several languages but is already used for field as a generic area and is less stable in the inherited canon.; linearna algebraična struktura: Too broad and omits the field axioms.
- **Uncertainty:** The complete phrase is editorial and the source does not list compatibility axioms; no stronger mathematical claim is made.; The r0001 record remains preserved as adverse provenance for the target/source locator mismatch.

## OLISV-T0188 · arithmetic.induction-schema

- **English lemma / sense:** induction schema — The infinite family of induction instances in first-order arithmetic, one for each permitted formula rather than one second-order axiom.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `shema indukcije`; lemma `shema indukcije`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Use/inflection:** Feminine head with genitive complement; genitive shemy indukcije. Use instance of the schema for one permitted formula.
- **Kirilična forma:** `схема индукције`
- **Rationale:** The phrase follows a near-unanimous international family and distinguishes an axiom schema from one axiom and from the earlier proof method structural induction.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.94 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0188-r0001`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: схема индукции; uk: схема індукції; pl: schemat indukcji; cs: schéma indukce; bcs: shema indukcije; шема индукције; bg: схема на индукцията.
- **Evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14890]; dictionary_id=18814; lemma=shema; gloss=scheme); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternatives:** indukcijska shema: The adjectival order is grammatical, but the genitive phrase aligns more uniformly with Russian, Ukrainian, Polish, Czech, Slovak, and the accepted edition's induction terminology.
- **Uncertainty:** The source itself warns that its displayed schema is simplified; parameters and side conditions are not fully stated.

## OLISV-T0187 · arithmetic.peano

- **English lemma / sense:** Peano arithmetic — The first-order arithmetic theory using zero, successor, addition, multiplication, order, and an induction schema.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `Peanova aritmetika`; lemma `Peanova aritmetika`; feminine proper-name noun phrase.
- **Use/inflection:** The possessive adjective agrees with aritmetika: genitive Peanovoj aritmetiky. Preserve the mathematician's surname as Peano in citations.
- **Kirilična forma:** `Пеанова аритметика`
- **Rationale:** The direct international head is unanimous, and Peanova supplies a compact possessive surface familiar to West and South Slavic readers while the comparison table preserves East-Slavic genitive phrasing.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.91 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0187-r0001`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: арифметика Пеано; uk: арифметика Пеано; pl: arytmetyka Peana; cs: Peanova aritmetika; bcs: Peanova aritmetika; Пеанова аритметика; bg: аритметика на Пеано.
- **Evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[1093]; dictionary_id=21256; lemma=aritmetika; gloss=arithmetics); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternatives:** aritmetika Peano: The indeclinable surname phrase is transparent and strong in East Slavic, but the possessive adjective integrates more naturally into the selected Interslavic syntax.
- **Uncertainty:** The source deliberately gives a simplified induction schema and does not state every parameter or side condition of a full presentation.

## OLISV-T0277 · arithmetic.power

- **English lemma / sense:** power — The exponent or degree to which a number is raised in an expression a to the b.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `stųpėnj`; lemma `stųpėnj`; masculine noun.
- **Use/inflection:** Masculine stųpėnj; genitive stųpnja. In the source construction use na ... stųpėnj after vozvesti.
- **Kirilična forma:** `ступењ`
- **Rationale:** Stųpėnj is the exact pinned dictionary head for degree and is used in the target's raise-to-a-power construction; the displayed exponentials fix the specialist exponent sense.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.9 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0277-r0001`.

- **Alternatives:** potencija: The international form is less broadly stable and can denote potency or exponentiation rather than the exponent itself.
- **Uncertainty:** The dictionary gloss says degree rather than exponent; the mathematical specialization is fixed by the source and target syntax.

## OLISV-T0333 · computability.artificial-intelligence

- **English lemma / sense:** artificial intelligence — The field of constructing or studying intelligent computational systems, named by the source as one home for investigations of nonclassical logics.

## OLISV-T0334 · computability.cognitive-science

- **English lemma / sense:** cognitive science — The interdisciplinary study of cognition, named by the source as another field in which the introduced logical systems may be investigated.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `kognitivna nauka`; lemma `kognitivna nauka`; feminine adjective-noun phrase.
- **Use/inflection:** Feminine nauka; genitive kognitivnoj nauky.
- **Kirilična forma:** `когнитивна наука`
- **Rationale:** Kognitivna nauka preserves the widely shared international adjective and pairs it with the direct pan-Slavic nauka head. Its transparent composition is preferable to importing the English field name or forcing a narrower psychology label.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.88 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0334-r0001`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: когнитивная наука; uk: когнітивна наука; pl: kognitywistyka; nauka kognitywna; cs: kognitivní věda; bcs: kognitivna znanost; когнитивна наука; bg: когнитивна наука.

## OLISV-T0023 · computability.computable

- **English lemma / sense:** computable — Capable of being computed by an effective procedure in the chosen model of computation.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `izčislimy`; lemma `izčislimy`; hard adjective.
- **Use/inflection:** Derived editorially from perfective izčisliti using the second-conjugation present passive ending -imy. Inflect regularly: izčislima funkcija, izčislimo množstvo, izčislimy funkcije.
- **Kirilična forma:** `изчислимы`
- **Rationale:** Izčislimy is a regular, compact formation from the directly attested compute verb and parallels the East/South Slavic modal adjective pattern. Its lexicalized specialist meaning is useful but not directly listed, so it remains provisional.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.66 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0023-r0001`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: вычислимый; uk: обчислюваний; pl: obliczalny; cs: vyčíslitelný; bcs: izračunljiv; bg: изчислим.
- **Evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260824 (wordList[2804]; dictionary_id=5879); ISV-GRAMMAR-VERBS (line 178); ISV-GRAMMAR-ADJ (lines 14-18); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternatives:** izčisljajemy: This regular imperfective present passive form means ‘being computed’ rather than the modal property ‘capable of computation’.
- **Uncertainty:** No bounded official dictionary row directly attests izčislimy in the computability-theory sense.; The exact Ukrainian, Polish, Czech, BCS, and Bulgarian adjectives are editorial comparanda rather than values copied from the cited verbal-base row.

## OLISV-T0022 · computability.computation

- **English lemma / sense:** computation — The process or result of calculating values according to an effective procedure.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `izčisljenje`; lemma `izčisljenje`; neuter verbal noun.
- **Use/inflection:** The dictionary source form izčisljeńje is normalized to standard/basic izčisljenje. Neuter noun; genitive singular izčisljenja. Use for a computational process or its result.
- **Kirilična forma:** `изчисљење`
- **Rationale:** The verbal noun is directly attested and strongly reinforced by inherited mathematical calculations; standardization only removes the optional soft-consonant diacritic.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.9 (high); `seed_preferred`; revision `OLISV-T0022-r0001`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: вычисление; uk: розрахунок; pl: obliczenie; cs: výpočet; bcs: obračun; bg: изчисление.

- **Alternatives:** kalkulacija: Kalkulacija is highly recognizable but more readily suggests a numerical or commercial calculation and lacks the inherited fit of izčisljenje.
- **Uncertainty:** The BCS dictionary columns favor obračun, which can carry a financial sense; this divergence is preserved in the comparanda.

## OLISV-T0335 · computability.computational-adjective

- **English lemma / sense:** computational — Pertaining to computation, used by the source both for properties of a logic and for a primary motivation for introducing systems.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `izčisliteljny`; lemma `izčisliteljny`; adjective.
- **Use/inflection:** Masculine izčisliteljny, feminine izčisliteljna, neuter izčisliteljno, plural izčisliteljne.
- **Kirilična forma:** `изчислитељны`
- **Rationale:** Izčisliteljny transparently derives an adjective from the edition's established izčisljati/izčisljenje computation family and remains distinct from izčislimy, which means computable. The source needs the broader relational adjective for properties and motivation.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.85 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0335-r0001`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: вычислительный; uk: обчислювальний; pl: obliczeniowy; cs: výpočetní; bcs: računski; рачунски; bg: изчислителен.
- **Evidence:** OL-FOL-BEYOND-OTHER-LOGICS (lines 19-20 and 33-36); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=5878; lemma=izčisljati; gloss=calculate, compute); ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (dictionary_id=3417; lemma=izčisljeńje; gloss=calculation); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard Latin alphabet and Cyrillic equivalence sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternatives:** izčislimy: Computable asserts that an object can be computed; computational only relates a property or motivation to computation, so substituting it would change the source's claim.
- **Uncertainty:** The exact adjective is productively derived rather than a standalone frozen lemma, and national languages divide between calculation and computer-oriented roots.

## OLISV-T0021 · computability.compute

- **English lemma / sense:** compute — To obtain the value of a function by an effective computational procedure.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `izčisljati`; lemma `izčisljati`; transitive imperfective verb.
- **Use/inflection:** Imperfective verb for the ongoing or general operation; perfective partner izčisliti for a completed computation. A function or algorithm is the grammatical subject and a value or function the object.
- **Kirilična forma:** `изчисљати`
- **Rationale:** Izčisljati is the only bounded dictionary verb explicitly glossed with compute and supplies a regular aspect partner for completed computations.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.86 (high); `seed_preferred`; revision `OLISV-T0021-r0001`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: вычислять; uk: розраховувати; pl: obliczać; cs: počítat; bcs: računati, izračunavati; bg: изчислявам.
- **Evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260824 (wordList[2803]; dictionary_id=5878); ISV-ORTHOGRAPHY (standard-alphabet and Cyrillic sections); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternatives:** kalkulovati: The international alternative is directly listed for calculate but is more readily associated with numerical or commercial calculation than general computability.
- **Uncertainty:** The dictionary marks this lemma type 2 and shows weaker West/South intelligibility than the international kalkulovati; the specialist compute sense motivates the selection.

## OLISV-T0025 · computability.decidable

- **English lemma / sense:** decidable — Computable as a set or relation, equivalently having an effective yes-or-no decision procedure.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `rěšimy`; lemma `rěšimy`; hard adjective.
- **Use/inflection:** Derived from rěšiti with the second-conjugation present passive ending -imy. Inflect regularly: rěšima relacija, rěšimo množstvo, rěšimy problemy.
- **Kirilična forma:** `рєшимы`
- **Rationale:** Rěšimy is morphologically regular, inherits a directly listed decide/determine base, and has an adjacent mathematical ‘solvable’ precedent in Noether. Open Logic’s definition fixes the algorithmic yes/no sense; the lexical extension stays provisional.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.64 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0025-r0001`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: разрешимый; uk: розв'язний; pl: rozstrzygalny; cs: rozhodnutelný; bcs: odlučiv; bg: разрешим.

- **Alternatives:** odlučimy: This would align more closely with BCS decide terminology but lacks the direct Interslavic base-row and inherited mathematical support available for rěšimy.
- **Uncertainty:** No bounded official dictionary row attests rěšimy specifically for computability-theoretic decidability.; The six specialist comparison adjectives are editorial comparanda; their cited rows supply only verbal bases.

## OLISV-T0024 · computability.turing-machine

- **English lemma / sense:** Turing machine — The abstract machine model that computes by state-governed operations on a tape.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `Turingova mašina`; lemma `Turingova mašina`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Use/inflection:** Mašina is the feminine head. Turingova is the nominative feminine singular possessive adjective in -ov and agrees with it; inflect both words as required by the clause.
- **Kirilična forma:** `Турингова машина`
- **Rationale:** The compound combines a directly attested, highly intelligible head with the official possessive-adjective pattern. It is concise and especially transparent to South Slavic readers, but remains provisional because the full eponym is not directly listed.

## OLISV-T0307 · logic.box-operator

- **English lemma / sense:** box operator — The modal operator Box, written □, asserting that its argument is necessarily true.

- **Alternatives:** svobodny za: A literal calque is gendered and can be misread as the already canonical free-occurrence relation rather than substitution admissibility.; dopustimy za podstanovku: Semantically precise but gendered; it would require agreement changes across term and prěměnna contexts.; podstavlivy: A compact coinage with no attestation in the frozen dictionary or accepted edition.

## OLISV-T0168 · logic.propositional-valuation

- **Alternatives:** vrednovanje: Recognizable in several South and West Slavic traditions, but in the current edition it already denotes the recursively extended evaluation function; using it for both levels would erase a source distinction.; prisvajanje istinnyh cěnnostij: Transparent but long, and prisvajanje is already the edition's canonical head for first-order variable assignment; the expansion remains available as an explanatory gloss rather than the recurring token.; valuacija: Internationally recognizable but lacks the selected candidate's direct pinned Interslavic dictionary attestation and its cross-family cognate support in this evidence set.
- **Uncertainty:** The direct dictionary row is polysemous and does not independently certify specialist logical usage.; Polish, Czech, and parts of South Slavic specialist traditions may prefer derivatives of value or evaluation rather than the selected ocěn- family.

## OLISV-T0075 · logic.substitution

- **English lemma / sense:** substitution — The recursive operation that replaces every occurrence of x in a term by t, and—when t is admissible for x in A—replaces all free occurrences of x in a formula A by t without variable capture.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `podstanovka`; lemma `podstanovka`; feminine noun.
- **Use/inflection:** Use podstanovka terma t vměsto prěměnnoj x. In a term it replaces every occurrence of x; in a formula it replaces all free occurrences only when t možno podstaviti vměsto x without capture. Generic zaměniti remains explanatory prose.
- **Kirilična forma:** `подстановка`
- **Rationale:** Podstanovka preserves exact inherited mathematical usage and the accepted Open Logic introduction. OLP-0158 now distinguishes the two recursive branches precisely: all occurrences in a term, but all free occurrences in a formula under the free-for condition. This append-only revision corrects the earlier compressed sense without changing the preferred head.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.78 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0075-r0002`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: подстановка; замена; uk: підстановка; заміна; pl: podstawienie; zastępstwo; cs: substituce; záměna; bcs: supstitucija; zamjena; bg: подстановка; замяна.
- **Evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[14249-14253], wordList[16508]; dictionary_id=3810,3811,19420,19421,1627,36101; lemma=zaměnjati, zaměniti, zastųpati, zastųpiti, zaměna); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternatives:** zaměna: Directly attested and useful in explanatory prose, but too broad for the recursively defined operation and may also imply exchange or swapping.; substitucija: Internationally recognizable but unsupported as an Interslavic lemma in the registered witnesses and unnecessary given inherited podstanovka.
- **Uncertainty:** The registered dictionary does not attest podstanovka.; The inherited Noether uses document mathematical substitution but not this exact recursive first-order operation.; No standalone verb entry is created; podstaviti occurs within OLISV-T0156 and accepted explanatory prose.

- **English lemma / sense:** Skolem's theorem — The first-order Skolemization result used here: a formula of the form for every x there exists y A(x,y) is satisfiable exactly when an expansion with a fresh function symbol satisfies for every x A(x,f(x)).

- **Confidence/status:** 0.87 (high); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0171-r0001`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: теорема Сколема; uk: теорема Сколема; pl: twierdzenie Skolema; cs: Skolemova věta; bcs: Skolemov teorem; Сколемова теорема; bg: теорема на Сколем.

- **Uncertainty:** The label Skolem's theorem is used for several related results in the literature; the canon sense is restricted to the exact statement in the pinned Open Logic unit.; No frozen dictionary row directly attests the proper-name phrase.

- **Rationale:** The exact international compound is transparent across all six comparison traditions and distinguishes the intended natural-number structure from Peano arithmetic as a theory.

## OLISV-T0176 · proof.contraposition

## OLISV-T0218 · set.russell-paradox

- **English lemma / sense:** Russell's paradox — The contradiction produced by unrestricted comprehension applied to the property of not belonging to oneself.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `Russellov paradoks`; lemma `Russellov paradoks`; masculine proper-name possessive phrase.
- **Use/inflection:** Masculine phrase; genitive Russellovogo paradoksa. Preserve the internationally established double l in the proper name.
- **Kirilična forma:** `Русселлов парадокс`

- **Alternatives:** paradoks Russella: The genitive construction is valid and common nationally, but the possessive form is the existing project head and produces a more compact stable label.
- **Uncertainty:** The paradox depends on unrestricted comprehension; it must not be attributed to the restricted separation schema recorded separately.

## OLISV-T0004 · set.union

- **English lemma / sense:** union; union over a set — The set containing exactly the elements belonging to at least one member of a specified family; this includes binary union and the operation denoted by union X.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `unija množstv`; lemma `unija`; feminine noun phrase.
- **Use/inflection:** Feminine noun; genitive unije. Use unija množstv for the general operation, unija X or formula notation for the union over a set X, and unija A i B for binary union.
- **Kirilična forma:** `унија множств`
- **Rationale:** The established international head remains unchanged. OLP-0170 explicitly introduces the family operation union X, so the revision broadens the recorded sense without conflating it with intersection or with the mereological sum introduced later in the same unit.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.72 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0004-r0002`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: объединение множеств; uk: об'єднання множин; pl: suma zbiorów; cs: sjednocení množin; bcs: unija skupova; bg: обединение на множества.

- **Alternatives:** sjedinjenje množstv: This Slavic-rooted alternative is semantically attractive and Noether attests sjedinenje, but no direct noun row or set-union attestation is present in the bounded official snapshot.
- **Uncertainty:** The choice between unija množstv and sjedinenje množstv needs stronger specialist Interslavic attestation; no human review is required to use this reversible provisional seed.

## OLISV-T0192 · set.urelement

- **English lemma / sense:** urelement — An object admitted as an element of sets while not itself being a set.
- **Medžuslovjansky:** `urelement`; lemma `urelement`; masculine noun.
- **Use/inflection:** Masculine noun; genitive urelementa, plural urelementy. Use only for a non-set object in a set-theoretic universe.
- **Kirilična forma:** `урелемент`
- **Rationale:** Urelement is the compact international technical term used by the source and is directly reconstructible from the established element head. Keeping it distinct avoids overloading atom, which has additional logical and mereological senses.
- **Confidence/status:** 0.73 (medium); `provisional`; revision `OLISV-T0192-r0001`.
- **Cross-Slavic comparanda:** ru: урэлемент; uk: урелемент; pl: urelement; cs: urelement; bcs: urelement; урелемент; bg: урелемент.
- **Evidence:** ISV-DICT-LIVE-20260825 (wordList[6006]; dictionary_id=17372; lemma=element; gloss=element); ISV-TRANSLIT-3.4.4 (package/src/transliterate/transliterate.ts; StandardCyrillic type=5, Standard flavorization=3)
- **Alternatives:** praelement: The calque is morphologically transparent but is not the source term and is less stable across specialist traditions than urelement.
- **Uncertainty:** No exact urelement entry occurs in the frozen dictionary response.; Atom is deliberately rejected because it is ambiguous across set theory, logic, physics, and mereology.

## Reproduction rule

Replay `CANON_INDEX.json` in order, verify every byte/hash pin, group records by `entry_id`, and select the greatest contiguous active revision. Never overwrite history: append a successor with explicit supersession, evidence and uncertainty.

Claim boundary: reversible project terminology, not official standardization, native certification, peer review, or completion of all 722 units.
